# 2027 New-Grad Timeline

Checked 2026-10-27. 1871 companies in `COMPANY_LIST.md`. Full data: `status.csv` (one row per company) and `postings.csv` (one row per open posting).

- **Apply now:** 1472 open postings not yet applied to, at 539 companies: 877 in your areas, 595 elsewhere (117 with a stated deadline)
- **Referrals / cold messages now:** 259 expected to open by 2026-12-22 or overdue vs last year
- **Tracker leads to confirm:** 0 listed by a GitHub tracker, company board not checked yet
- **Later / unknown timing:** 194
- **Companies in TRACKER.md:** 88 (101 applications); other open postings at these companies still appear under Apply now
- **No US new-grad SWE track found:** 832
- **Dropped (citizenship, green card, or clearance required):** 22 — kept in `status.csv` as `excluded`

"Apply now" only counts postings seen on the company's own job board. GitHub trackers (SimplifyJobs, speedyapply, vanshb03) are used for leads and last-cycle dates. Posted is the earliest date the posting showed, blank when it showed none; Discovered is the first day a run saw its link.
